## WHY THIS PASS EXISTS, AND WHAT CLOSES THE CHAIN

Pass 2 (60 rows) had six clean sweeps.  The 78-row repair killed them; pass 2's builder then

**Two consecutive passes on one class means the repair owes the CLASS, not the cell**: this
diff enumerates the full space from the guard's own inputs (len, writeflag, relative_addr ->
block, idata, d->warned), closes every cell in ONE diff, and writes into the contract which
cells are covered, which are argued same-channel, which are equivalent by construction with
their warrant, and which are unreachable.  **Your job is to VERIFY THAT ENUMERATION** -- is the

smallest edit that breaks the property and passes all 96 rows if one exists.  A closing zero
must carry its denominator: say which cells and shapes you swept.

## N. PREMISES ALREADY SETTLED, RE-INLINED

Settled by passes 1-3 (`panel_20260903_121558`, `panel_20260903_152458`, and the measurement
artifacts under `regress/artifacts/pcc2widthkills/`), warrants inline:

1. **The width is LEGAL** -- `mvme_pcctworeg.h`: "bytes, two-bytes, or four-bytes".
2. **`memory_readmax64` composes BIG-ENDIAN**: `(uint8_t) idata' is the HIGHEST-address lane;
   a bare deletion stores the neighbour's byte.  MEASURED (`M_DELETE' -> `rb genctl' 0xbb).
3. **Reachability:** every non-byte m88k access is alignment-checked before memory_rw
   (`#ifndef LS_1 ... m88k_exception(MISALIGNED)' then return) and `relative_addr %= 0x40'
   keeps parity.  SIX even blocks (GENCTL 0x02, TxCTL 0x16, TxICR 0x1a, SCCERR 0x1c, SCCTX
   0x1e, IPL 0x3e) are GUEST-reachable; EIGHT odd OFFSETS -- the six odd-only blocks VECBASE
   0x03, PSCALEADJ 0x15, SCCICR 0x1d, SCCRX 0x1f, SCSIICR 0x2f, MASK 0x3f, plus the T1CTL 0x17
   / T1ICR 0x1b labels of the shared TxCTL/TxICR blocks -- are OPERATOR-only.  Byte accesses
   (`st.b', `put b') are never alignment-checked.
4. **The operator path reaches the SAME guard**: `put h' warns, then `store_16bit_word' ->
   ONE `memory_rw(len 2)' -> the same arm.  `put w' / `put d' likewise give len 4 / 8.
   **A debugger READ does not reach the arm**: `memory_rw' dispatches a device only if
   `(!no_exceptions || (flags & DM_READS_HAVE_NO_SIDE_EFFECTS))' and pcc2 registers
   `DM_DEFAULT' -- so the six odd blocks' READ-side cells are unreachable by construction.
5. **Arm bodies** (inlined below): VECBASE stores `idata & 0xf0' (+ HUH print); SCCICR
   `& 0x1f'; SCCRX and SCSIICR `& 0xdf'; each of those four then (except VECBASE) calls
   `reassert_interrupts()'.  PSCALEADJ stores nothing, prints "ignored (value ...)".  IPL's
   write body stores nothing and prints "write to read-only PCCTWO_IPL; ignored" once per
   process (#119 static).  SCCERR's write body is `= 0' and nothing in the model stores a
   nonzero SCCERR.  TxCTL `& 3'; TxICR `&= ~0x17 |= idata & 0x17'.  GENCTL and MASK store the
   byte and reassert.  SCCRXIACK has NO width guard and never had one: its body ignores idata.
6. **`fatal()' returns**; the announce is once per block per instance via `d->warned', a
   32-bit field with twelve bits assigned, written ONLY inside `pcc2_width_decline()'.
   `reassert_interrupts()' exits the host only on `GENCTL & 0x04' (pcc2c040kill, filed);
   every GENCTL value in the probe keeps that bit clear.  Every probe session is a fresh
   `pty.fork'/`execv' of the emulator.
7. **The ruling, applied to all twelve now**: clause (d) is a FLOOR; a disclosed asymmetry
   cannot discharge it.  Announce-then-fall-through leaves every `dcl' row and `latch all'
   green by construction, so its only channel is a readback (storing arms) or the arm's own
   print (print-only arms).
8. **Pass-3 roster**: eight answered (codex, kimi, grok, glm, deepseek, minimax, opus, fable5);
   agy DELIBERATELY NOT FIRED (reserved for measurement).  Three seats measured.

## THE CONTRACT (the probe's docstring, VERBATIM, as it now stands)

```
pcc2widthkills DETECTOR -- the twelve width-guard blocks in DEVICE_ACCESS(pcc2).

WHAT THE FIX DID.  Twelve `if (len != 1) { fatal(...); exit(1); }' blocks (ten kills and
two #119 function-static latches) ended the host on a guest-chosen access width.  The
tree's own header says PCC2 registers "can be accessed as bytes, two-bytes, or
four-bytes", so the width is LEGAL.  The fix is a DIRECTION SPLIT:

    READS   ACCEPT -- the memcpy above the switch already served them, raw bytes in
            address order, width-correct by construction.  The guard discarded a
            correct answer.
    WRITES  DECLINE + per-instance latched announce -- every write arm uses `idata',
            the big-endian-composed value, which is "the addressed byte" ONLY at
            len==1.  Deleting the guard would store the NEIGHBOUR's lane.

CONTRACT (vacuity-floor clause (e)) -- what this detector is designed to catch:

  CATCHES
    * reinstatement of the kill at any of the SIX GUEST-reachable blocks (survival
      rows paired with a liveness observable, because exit status cannot tell
      "declined and quit" from "died with status 0");
    * *** pass 2: the two FOLDED-IN arms, IPL and MASK, had NO ROW -- twelve blocks
      converted, ten given rows, and the shipped contract did not say so.  A seat
      found it by sibling parity: MASK is GENCTL's twin and had none of GENCTL's rows.
      Repaired: IPL is EVEN and therefore the SIs slip, blocks vs labels.
 GENCTL, T2CTL, T2ICR, SCCERR, SCCTX = 0x02, 0x16, 0x1a, 0x1c, 0x1e
 VECBASE, PSCALEADJ, T1CTL, T1ICR, SCCICR, SCCRX, SCSIICR = 0x03, 0x15, 0x17, 0x1b, 0x1d, 0x1f, 0x2f
+#  pcc2 pass 2: THE TWO FOLDED-IN ARMS HAD NO ROW AT ALL.  A seat found that the probe
+#  touched no offset at 0x3e or 0x3f -- twelve blocks converted, ten given rows.  MASK
+#  is GENCTL's byte-identical twin (store byte, reassert); GENCTL's null-repair was
+#  caught by `rb genctl', MASK's by nothing, a 60/60 sweep.  AND IPL IS EVEN: it is the
+#  SIXTH guest-reachable block, not a seventh operator one.
+IPL, MASK = 0x3e, 0x3f
 CHIPID = 0x00

 def program(insns, at=CODE):
+    #  `at' lets a session run TWO programs: a second sequence at the SAME address finds
+    #  pc already there and the assignment is skipped (the Addr hazard, designed out in
+    #  the sgi probes with Addr.take()).  Callers needing two pass at=CODE + 0x100.
     words = list(insns) + [I_BR_SELF, 0]
     cmds = ["put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (at + 4 * i, w) for i, w in enumerate(words)]
     return cmds + ["pc=0x%x" % at, "step %d" % len(insns)]

+t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x11", r6="0x0022")

+#  `latch same' and `latch two' pin ONE pair out of 66: every other session writes at most
+#  one BLOCK, so a bit shared by any other pair announces normally and no row moves.
+#  MEASURED by the builder: aliasing SCCTX onto SCCRX's bit -- a ONE-CHARACTER edit --
+#  left the 60-row detector at 60/60.  ONE OFFSET PER BLOCK: TxCTL serves T1CTL+T2CTL
+#  and TxICR serves T1ICR+T2ICR, so twelve LABELS would give only ten announces -- the
+#  same units slip that let "five even blocks + seven odd ones = twelve" read as all
+#  twelve blocks.  This row pins the CARDINALITY of the latch: abody only prints "ignored (value ...)".  A landed
+#      wide write therefore prints that line -- the message is the only channel, and the
+#      row counts it at ZERO.
+for nm, off in (("sccicr", SCCICR), ("sccrx", SCCRX), ("scsiicr", SCSIICR)):

## PRETABLE_amendments.out (Sections 5 and 6)

```
PRETABLE AMENDMENTS -- pcc2widthkills, killtable2_20260903.  Split out of PRETABLE.out on pass 3.


SECTION 5 -- AMENDMENT, written after table-2 PART 1 (9 mutants + BASE, all killed) and
BEFORE PART 2.  Section 2's "sentence owed" was RULED A FLOOR BREACH, not a disclosure.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
The measuring seat, running its own pass 3 against the 78-row detector, asked whether the
disclosed asymmetry (PSCALEADJ/SCCICR/SCCRX/SCSIICR: no readback, so a guard that ANNOUNCES
and then lets the halfword land is invisible) can be discharged by clause (e).  Ruling: NO.
Clause (d) is a FLOOR -- "the guard kept, its effect removed" -- and the fall-through shape at
those arms is exactly that.  Clause (e) governs claims ABOVE the floor; a disclosure cannot
place a detector below it, or every floor breach becomes dischargeable by a sentence.  The
CHANNEL rule in Section 4 already said as much: a `dcl' row cannot vary under a mutant that
keeps announcing, so at those four arms it was the WRONG channel for this shape.

Rows added (part 2 measures them):
  rb sccicr / rb sccrx / rb scsiicr   set 0x1f by byte, decline put h 0xaab8, read back 0x1f
                                      M_FALL_* lands 0x18 (& 0x1f) / 0x98 (& 0xdf) and REASSERTS
  op pscale noig                      PSCALEADJ's body stores nothing and only prints; count of
                                      "PSCALEADJ ignored" in the wide-write session == 0.
                                      M_FALL_PSCALEADJ prints it once.
Blast radius is NOT uniform across the four (the seat's point): three of them put the
neighbour's lane into an INTERRUPT CONTROL register and reassert; one prints.  One sentence
had covered all four, which was too coarse as well as below the floor.


SECTION 6 -- PASS 3: THE CLASS CLOSURE.  Written after pass 3's seats landed and BEFORE
part 3 of the table.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Pass 3 found the SAME class a third time: the fable5 seat MEASURED announce-then-fall-through
at VECBASE as 82/82 (its `pcc2p3_fable/probe_mfall_vecbase.out`).  `rb vecbase' is GENCTL's
neighbour-lane check (st.b VECBASE; st.h GENCTL; ld.bu VECBASE) and Section 2 above credited
it as VECBASE's own readback -- a wrong record that hid the cell.  Independently, glm and grok
named IPL: its write body prints "write to read-only PCCTWO_IPL; ignored" once per process and
stores nothing, so a fall-through has a MESSAGE channel exactly as PSCALEADJ does -- and the
fable5 seat had called that cell a no-op, the same direction the builder was wrong about
PSCALEADJ.  SCCERR's fall-through cell is EQUIVALENT BY CONSTRUCTION: the arm stores 0 and no
code in the model stores a nonzero SCCERR (grep), so decline and land are the same state.

The enumeration (12 blocks x 6 shapes) is now written into the probe's CONTRACT with a status
per cell; the two open cells get rows:
  rb vecbase own   st.b 0x50 at VECBASE; put h 0xaab8 at VECBASE; ld.bu -> 0x50.
                   M_FALL_VECBASE lands 0xb0 (0xaab8 & 0xf0) and also prints HUH.
  hwr ipl noig     st.h 0x0303 at IPL (guest-reachable); count of the read-only print == 0.
                   M_FALL_IPL prints it once.
The codex seat added two shapes the six-shape list did not have, both closable at all twelve
blocks by one cardinality row each:
  latch all w      `len != 1' -> `len == 2' (LEN's mirror) declines every halfword row and lets
                   a WORD land; twelve `put w' writes, twelve announces.  M_LEN2_VECBASE -> 11.
  acc all (+lv)    the contract claimed byte writes land "at every reachable arm" while acc
                   rows existed at five blocks.  Twelve legal `put b' writes, ZERO declines.
                   M_GROW_PSCALEADJ (`len != 1' -> `len != 0' at an arm with no acc row) -> 1.
The Opus reader seat MEASURED a ninth shape, 82/82, ONE character: `writeflag == MEM_WRITE'
-> `<= MEM_WRITE', the direction split's own conjunct nulled, so wide READS are declined,
announced as writes, skip the arm's read body and consume the latch bit.  No session holding
a wide read carried a decline-count row.  Closed with ZERO new sessions:
  hrd <block> nod  (six even blocks) and wrd sccerr nod: t.count(DECL) == 0 on the existing
                   wide-read sessions.  M_RDDECL_ALL (all twelve conjuncts) -> seven reds.
  The six odd blocks' read-side cells are UNREACHABLE by construction (guest wide reads at
  odd offsets alignment-fault before memory_rw; debugger reads do not dispatch to DM_DEFAULT
  devices) and are disclosed in the contract.
The T1 labels (deepseek): TxCTL/TxICR are SHARED blocks whose readbacks sat at the T2 offsets;
`rb t1ctl' / `rb t1icr' (operator path, own readbacks) close the T1 half.  M_FALL_TXCTL
(break removed at the shared block) -> rb t2ctl AND rb t1ctl both red.  SCCRXIACK (glm): the
thirteenth arm, never width-guarded, body ignores idata -- named in the contract as excluded.
Part 3 measures BASE 96/96, M_FALL_VECBASE, M_FALL_IPL, M_LEN2_VECBASE, M_GROW_PSCALEADJ,
M_RDDECL_ALL, M_FALL_TXCTL.  Every other cell keeps its measured or same-channel status from
parts 1-2 and the builder's pass 3/3b/3c: adding rows cannot un-kill a mutant.  A closing
pass 4 verifies THIS enumeration (12 x 9 plus the two T1 labels, written into the contract);
it does not hunt cells.


SECTION 7 -- PART 3 MEASURED (killtable2_20260903/part3/, part3b/), and what it corrected.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Part 3 (96-row instrument): BASE 95/96 -- `rb t1ctl' red on the fix by a STRING mismatch (the
row wanted "0x03", the debugger prints "0x3"; the value was right).  Fixed; part 3b BASE 96/96.
Kills, each on exactly the row Section 6 named (the artifact row subtracted):
  M_FALL_VECBASE    rb vecbase own          M_FALL_IPL       hwr ipl noig
  M_LEN2_VECBASE    latch all w             M_RDDECL_ALL     all seven hrd/wrd * nod (88/96)
  M_FALL_TXCTL      rb t2ctl AND rb t1ctl   (part 3b, BASE full: the T1 cell measured)
ESCAPE: M_GROW_PSCALEADJ 95/96 (part 3) and 96/96 (part 3b) -- `acc all' did not move, WITH
the mutation PROVEN in the source (1 x `len != 0') and the binary newer than the source (3b's
proof lines).  So the row could not see the arm: the debugger's `put b' calls memory_rw with
NO_EXCEPTIONS, and memory_rw.c's device dispatch is `if (!no_exceptions || (flags &
DM_READS_HAVE_NO_SIDE_EFFECTS))'; pcc2 registers DM_DEFAULT, so a NO_EXCEPTIONS byte write is
never dispatched.  `put h/w/d' go through store_Nbit_word and do reach the arms (every other
cardinality row worked).  THE ROW WAS VACUOUS BY CONSTRUCTION -- the CHANNEL rule's failure
condition, caught by the table rather than at writing time.  `acc all' is now twelve GUEST
`st.b' stores (legal at every offset), and part 3c re-measures BASE + M_GROW_PSCALEADJ.
The contract now states the `put b' fact so no future row rests on it.

## OPERATIONAL RULE FOR EVERY SEAT (appended by panel.sh, not by the author)

**Do not run `run.sh`, any `gate_*.sh`, or the battery.** Every measurement you need is
inlined above. A gate takes minutes -- longer than your response budget -- and a second
concurrent invocation corrupts the operator run in progress, because the gates share one
log directory. A seat that runs a gate produces no review AND may invalidate someone
else s. Building and running a single detector or mutant in your own temp directory is
fine and encouraged; driving the harness is not.

If you want a measurement that is not inlined, NAME IT and say why. Naming it is worth
far more to this panel than a timeout.

      seven named-text `dcl' rows and `latch all' pin cardinality and the rest of the names.

      THE SHARED BLOCKS.  TxCTL serves T1CTL (0x17, odd) and T2CTL (0x16, even); TxICR
      serves T1ICR (0x1b) and T2ICR (0x1a).  The blocks' readbacks above are at the T2
      offsets; a seat noted that a fall-through reaching only the T1 labels (the block
      split in two) would leave `op t1ctl dcl' at 1 with nothing reading T1CTL back.  So
      the T1 labels carry their own operator-path readbacks, `rb t1ctl' and `rb t1icr'.
      THE THIRTEENTH ARM.  SCCRXIACK has NO width guard and never had one: its body
      ignores `idata' (it clears a bit in SCCRX and reasserts), so its effect is
      width-invariant by construction and it is not a member of the twelve.  It is named
      here so the census is not read as an omission.
      SESSIONS.  Every session() is a fresh pty.fork/execv of the emulator, so no latch
      state survives from one row to the next; `latch all' counts one process.

  DOES NOT CATCH, stated so an escape is outside the contract rather than an argument
    * per-instance vs process-global latch state: mvme187 instantiates ONE pcc2, so no
      session can hold two instances.  The bitfield is per-instance by construction;
      nothing here measures it;
    * pcc2c040kill -- a LEGAL byte write of 0x04 to GENCTL still ends the host in
      reassert_interrupts().  Every GENCTL lane value here keeps bit 0x04 CLEAR so no
      row dies for that reason;
    * pcc2defaultkill -- an unlisted offset (0x06, 0x14, 0x18, 0x2c ...) still ends the
      host in `default:';
    * pcc2counter8 -- the counter arm's raw memcpy of an 8-byte write;
    * what real hardware does with the LANES of a wide write (not on disk);
    * the SCCERR word-write's stored value: the arm always stores 0, so a decline and
      an accept are value-identical there and the row's channel is the message.

Sentinel: PCC2W_PROBE_DONE

## THE ENCLOSING CODE (the helper by brace count; the arms whose rows are new)

```c
static void pcc2_width_decline(struct pcc2_data *d, int bit, const char *name, int len)
{
	if (!(d->warned & (1u << bit))) {
		d->warned |= (1u << bit);
		fatal("[ pcc2: unimplemented len=%i write to %s -- declined."
		    "  (once) ]\n", len, name);
	}
}

	case PCCTWO_VECBASE:
		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_VECBASE_LEN, "PCCTWO_VECBASE", len);
			break;
		}
		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] = idata & 0xf0;
			if (idata & ~0xf0)
				fatal("[ pcc2: HUH? write to PCCTWO_VECBASE"
				    " with value 0x%02x. ]\n", (int) idata);
		}
		break;

	case PCCTWO_T1CMP:
	case PCCTWO_T2CMP:
	case PCCTWO_T1COUNT:
	case PCCTWO_T2COUNT:
		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE &&
		    relative_addr + len <= PCC2_SIZE)	/* OB-11: end-span */
			memcpy(d->pcctwo_reg + relative_addr, data, len);
		break;

	case PCCTWO_IPL:
		/*  pcc2widthkills: was a #119 `static int w' -- process-global, and it
		    said "ignored" about a READ the memcpy above had already served.
		    Now per-instance, write-only, like the ten blocks above.  */
		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_IPL_LEN, "PCCTWO_IPL", len);
			break;
		}
		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			static int w = 0;	/* #119: was exit(1) (IPL is read-only) */
			if (!w) { fatal("[ pcc2: write to read-only PCCTWO_IPL; "
			    "ignored ]\n"); w = 1; }
		} else {
			if (d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_IPL] == 0) {
				static int w = 0;	/* #119: was exit(1) */
				if (!w) { fatal("[ pcc2: IPL == 0 on read ]\n");
				    w = 1; }
			}
		}
		break;

	case PCCTWO_MASK:
		/*  pcc2widthkills: was a #119 `static int w' -- process-global, and it
		    said "ignored" about a READ the memcpy above had already served.
		    Now per-instance, write-only, like the ten blocks above.  */
		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_MASK_LEN, "PCCTWO_MASK", len);
			break;
		}
		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] = idata;
			reassert_interrupts(d);
		}
		break;

	case PCCTWO_T2CTL:
	case PCCTWO_T1CTL:
		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_TXCTL_LEN, "PCCTWO_TxCTL", len);
			break;
		}
		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			/*  PCC2_TCTL_CEN and PCC2_TCTL_COC:  */
			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] &= 0xfc;
			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] |= (idata & 3);

			if (idata & PCC2_TCTL_COVF)
				d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] &=
				    ~(PCC2_TCTL_OVF | PCC2_TCTL_COVF);

			if (idata & ~7)
				fatal("[ pcc2: HUH? write to PCCTWO_TxCTL"
				    " with value 0x%02x. ]\n", (int) idata);
		}
		break;

	case PCCTWO_T2ICR:
	case PCCTWO_T1ICR:
		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_TXICR_LEN, "PCCTWO_TxICR", len);
			break;
		}
		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] &= ~0x17;
			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] |= (idata & 0x17);

			if (relative_addr == PCCTWO_T1ICR &&
			    !(idata & PCC2_TTIRQ_IEN))
				d->pending_timer1_interrupts = 0;
			if (relative_addr == PCCTWO_T2ICR &&
			    !(idata & PCC2_TTIRQ_IEN))
				d->pending_timer2_interrupts = 0;

			if (relative_addr == PCCTWO_T1ICR && (idata & PCC2_TTIRQ_ICLR)
			    && d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] & PCC2_TTIRQ_INT
			    && d->pending_timer1_interrupts > 0)
				d->pending_timer1_interrupts --;

			if (relative_addr == PCCTWO_T2ICR && (idata & PCC2_TTIRQ_ICLR)
			    && d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] & PCC2_TTIRQ_INT
			    && d->pending_timer2_interrupts > 0)
				d->pending_timer2_interrupts --;

			if (idata & PCC2_TTIRQ_ICLR)
				d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] &= ~PCC2_TTIRQ_INT;

			if (idata & 0xe0)
				fatal("[ pcc2: HUH? write to PCCTWO_TxICR"
				    " with value 0x%02x. ]\n", (int) idata);

			reassert_interrupts(d);
		}
		break;
```

## PRETABLE_amendments.out (Sections 5 and 6)

```
PRETABLE AMENDMENTS -- pcc2widthkills, killtable2_20260903.  Split out of PRETABLE.out on pass 3.


SECTION 5 -- AMENDMENT, written after table-2 PART 1 (9 mutants + BASE, all killed) and
BEFORE PART 2.  Section 2's "sentence owed" was RULED A FLOOR BREACH, not a disclosure.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
The measuring seat, running its own pass 3 against the 78-row detector, asked whether the
disclosed asymmetry (PSCALEADJ/SCCICR/SCCRX/SCSIICR: no readback, so a guard that ANNOUNCES
and then lets the halfword land is invisible) can be discharged by clause (e).  Ruling: NO.
Clause (d) is a FLOOR -- "the guard kept, its effect removed" -- and the fall-through shape at
those arms is exactly that.  Clause (e) governs claims ABOVE the floor; a disclosure cannot
place a detector below it, or every floor breach becomes dischargeable by a sentence.  The
CHANNEL rule in Section 4 already said as much: a `dcl' row cannot vary under a mutant that
keeps announcing, so at those four arms it was the WRONG channel for this shape.

Rows added (part 2 measures them):
  rb sccicr / rb sccrx / rb scsiicr   set 0x1f by byte, decline put h 0xaab8, read back 0x1f
                                      M_FALL_* lands 0x18 (& 0x1f) / 0x98 (& 0xdf) and REASSERTS
  op pscale noig                      PSCALEADJ's body stores nothing and only prints; count of
                                      "PSCALEADJ ignored" in the wide-write session == 0.
                                      M_FALL_PSCALEADJ prints it once.
Blast radius is NOT uniform across the four (the seat's point): three of them put the
neighbour's lane into an INTERRUPT CONTROL register and reassert; one prints.  One sentence
had covered all four, which was too coarse as well as below the floor.


SECTION 6 -- PASS 3: THE CLASS CLOSURE.  Written after pass 3's seats landed and BEFORE
part 3 of the table.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Pass 3 found the SAME class a third time: the fable5 seat MEASURED announce-then-fall-through
at VECBASE as 82/82 (its `pcc2p3_fable/probe_mfall_vecbase.out`).  `rb vecbase' is GENCTL's
neighbour-lane check (st.b VECBASE; st.h GENCTL; ld.bu VECBASE) and Section 2 above credited
it as VECBASE's own readback -- a wrong record that hid the cell.  Independently, glm and grok
named IPL: its write body prints "write to read-only PCCTWO_IPL; ignored" once per process and
stores nothing, so a fall-through has a MESSAGE channel exactly as PSCALEADJ does -- and the
fable5 seat had called that cell a no-op, the same direction the builder was wrong about
PSCALEADJ.  SCCERR's fall-through cell is EQUIVALENT BY CONSTRUCTION: the arm stores 0 and no
code in the model stores a nonzero SCCERR (grep), so decline and land are the same state.

The enumeration (12 blocks x 6 shapes) is now written into the probe's CONTRACT with a status
per cell; the two open cells get rows:
  rb vecbase own   st.b 0x50 at VECBASE; put h 0xaab8 at VECBASE; ld.bu -> 0x50.
                   M_FALL_VECBASE lands 0xb0 (0xaab8 & 0xf0) and also prints HUH.
  hwr ipl noig     st.h 0x0303 at IPL (guest-reachable); count of the read-only print == 0.
                   M_FALL_IPL prints it once.
The codex seat added two shapes the six-shape list did not have, both closable at all twelve
blocks by one cardinality row each:
  latch all w      `len != 1' -> `len == 2' (LEN's mirror) declines every halfword row and lets
                   a WORD land; twelve `put w' writes, twelve announces.  M_LEN2_VECBASE -> 11.
  acc all (+lv)    the contract claimed byte writes land "at every reachable arm" while acc
                   rows existed at five blocks.  Twelve legal `put b' writes, ZERO declines.
                   M_GROW_PSCALEADJ (`len != 1' -> `len != 0' at an arm with no acc row) -> 1.
The Opus reader seat MEASURED a ninth shape, 82/82, ONE character: `writeflag == MEM_WRITE'
-> `<= MEM_WRITE', the direction split's own conjunct nulled, so wide READS are declined,
announced as writes, skip the arm's read body and consume the latch bit.  No session holding
a wide read carried a decline-count row.  Closed with ZERO new sessions:
  hrd <block> nod  (six even blocks) and wrd sccerr nod: t.count(DECL) == 0 on the existing
                   wide-read sessions.  M_RDDECL_ALL (all twelve conjuncts) -> seven reds.
  The six odd blocks' read-side cells are UNREACHABLE by construction (guest wide reads at
  odd offsets alignment-fault before memory_rw; debugger reads do not dispatch to DM_DEFAULT
  devices) and are disclosed in the contract.
The T1 labels (deepseek): TxCTL/TxICR are SHARED blocks whose readbacks sat at the T2 offsets;
`rb t1ctl' / `rb t1icr' (operator path, own readbacks) close the T1 half.  M_FALL_TXCTL
(break removed at the shared block) -> rb t2ctl AND rb t1ctl both red.  SCCRXIACK (glm): the
thirteenth arm, never width-guarded, body ignores idata -- named in the contract as excluded.
Part 3 measures BASE 96/96, M_FALL_VECBASE, M_FALL_IPL, M_LEN2_VECBASE, M_GROW_PSCALEADJ,
M_RDDECL_ALL, M_FALL_TXCTL.  Every other cell keeps its measured or same-channel status from
parts 1-2 and the builder's pass 3/3b/3c: adding rows cannot un-kill a mutant.  A closing
pass 4 verifies THIS enumeration (12 x 9 plus the two T1 labels, written into the contract);
it does not hunt cells.


SECTION 7 -- PART 3 MEASURED (killtable2_20260903/part3/, part3b/), and what it corrected.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Part 3 (96-row instrument): BASE 95/96 -- `rb t1ctl' red on the fix by a STRING mismatch (the
row wanted "0x03", the debugger prints "0x3"; the value was right).  Fixed; part 3b BASE 96/96.
Kills, each on exactly the row Section 6 named (the artifact row subtracted):
  M_FALL_VECBASE    rb vecbase own          M_FALL_IPL       hwr ipl noig
  M_LEN2_VECBASE    latch all w             M_RDDECL_ALL     all seven hrd/wrd * nod (88/96)
  M_FALL_TXCTL      rb t2ctl AND rb t1ctl   (part 3b, BASE full: the T1 cell measured)
ESCAPE: M_GROW_PSCALEADJ 95/96 (part 3) and 96/96 (part 3b) -- `acc all' did not move, WITH
the mutation PROVEN in the source (1 x `len != 0') and the binary newer than the source (3b's
proof lines).  So the row could not see the arm: the debugger's `put b' calls memory_rw with
NO_EXCEPTIONS, and memory_rw.c's device dispatch is `if (!no_exceptions || (flags &
DM_READS_HAVE_NO_SIDE_EFFECTS))'; pcc2 registers DM_DEFAULT, so a NO_EXCEPTIONS byte write is
never dispatched.  `put h/w/d' go through store_Nbit_word and do reach the arms (every other
cardinality row worked).  THE ROW WAS VACUOUS BY CONSTRUCTION -- the CHANNEL rule's failure
condition, caught by the table rather than at writing time.  `acc all' is now twelve GUEST
`st.b' stores (legal at every offset), and part 3c re-measures BASE + M_GROW_PSCALEADJ.
The contract now states the `put b' fact so no future row rests on it.

```

## OPERATIONAL RULE FOR EVERY SEAT (appended by panel.sh, not by the author)

**Do not run `run.sh`, any `gate_*.sh`, or the battery.** Every measurement you need is
inlined above. A gate takes minutes -- longer than your response budget -- and a second
concurrent invocation corrupts the operator run in progress, because the gates share one
log directory. A seat that runs a gate produces no review AND may invalidate someone
else s. Building and running a single detector or mutant in your own temp directory is
fine and encouraged; driving the harness is not.

If you want a measurement that is not inlined, NAME IT and say why. Naming it is worth
far more to this panel than a timeout.

# pcc2widthkills -- PASS 4: the CLASS CLOSURE.  Detector 82 -> 96 rows; the whole 12-block x 9-shape space is written into the contract with a status per cell.  Product diff is comments only.

## READ THIS FIRST -- PLEASE DO NOT SIMPLY RATIFY

A recent panel had seven of nine seats confirm a premise this brief's author stated wrongly,
and THIS round's pass 3 found the author had (a) mis-credited a row in his own parity table,
(b) applied his own ruling to four arms when it convicted five, (c) never walked the one guard
input the fix itself introduced, and (d) claimed accept-side coverage carried by five rows.
**Check the premises, not just the derivations.**  Where I state something as fact, ask
whether it is true.  A seat that re-derives a premise from something this brief did NOT give
it is worth more than a seat that agrees.

**THE TREE UNDER REVIEW IS `GXEMUL-SEC/src/` and `GXEMUL-SEC/regress/`, UNCOMMITTED on top of
`b78bb0f`.**  Three pristine-2021 copies under the project root are NOT this fork.  If your line
numbers disagree with mine by hundreds you are reading one of them -- say so.

**If you cannot read files that is expected** -- every fact you need is inlined.  Name any
premise you cannot settle from this packet rather than guessing at it.

**Where this brief states an ARITHMETIC claim -- a lane value under a mask, a count of blocks,
a latch-bit cardinality -- ENUMERATE it rather than reasoning about it.**  And ENUMERATE THE
RIGHT DOMAIN: the m88k core alignment-checks every non-byte access BEFORE memory_rw, and the
debugger's `put' path does not.

**VERDICT LINE FIRST**, so your answer survives truncation.

## WHY THIS PASS EXISTS, AND WHAT CLOSES THE CHAIN

Pass 2 (60 rows) had six clean sweeps.  The 78-row repair killed them; pass 2's builder then
found four odd-arm fall-throughs sweeping 78; the 82-row repair killed those.  Pass 3 found
the SAME class a third time (VECBASE, IPL) and three shapes the six-shape list never named.
**Two consecutive passes on one class means the repair owes the CLASS, not the cell**: this
diff enumerates the full space from the guard's own inputs (len, writeflag, relative_addr ->
block, idata, d->warned), closes every cell in ONE diff, and writes into the contract which
cells are covered, which are argued same-channel, which are equivalent by construction with
their warrant, and which are unreachable.  **Your job is to VERIFY THAT ENUMERATION** -- is the
domain right, is every cell's status right, is any status a rationalisation -- and to name the
smallest edit that breaks the property and passes all 96 rows if one exists.  A closing zero
must carry its denominator: say which cells and shapes you swept.

## N. PREMISES ALREADY SETTLED, RE-INLINED

Settled by passes 1-3 (`panel_20260903_121558`, `panel_20260903_152458`, and the measurement
artifacts under `regress/artifacts/pcc2widthkills/`), warrants inline:

1. **The width is LEGAL** -- `mvme_pcctworeg.h`: "bytes, two-bytes, or four-bytes".
2. **`memory_readmax64` composes BIG-ENDIAN**: `(uint8_t) idata' is the HIGHEST-address lane;
   a bare deletion stores the neighbour's byte.  MEASURED (`M_DELETE' -> `rb genctl' 0xbb).
3. **Reachability:** every non-byte m88k access is alignment-checked before memory_rw
   (`#ifndef LS_1 ... m88k_exception(MISALIGNED)' then return) and `relative_addr %= 0x40'
   keeps parity.  SIX even blocks (GENCTL 0x02, TxCTL 0x16, TxICR 0x1a, SCCERR 0x1c, SCCTX
   0x1e, IPL 0x3e) are GUEST-reachable; EIGHT odd OFFSETS -- the six odd-only blocks VECBASE
   0x03, PSCALEADJ 0x15, SCCICR 0x1d, SCCRX 0x1f, SCSIICR 0x2f, MASK 0x3f, plus the T1CTL 0x17
   / T1ICR 0x1b labels of the shared TxCTL/TxICR blocks -- are OPERATOR-only.  Byte accesses
   (`st.b', `put b') are never alignment-checked.
4. **The operator path reaches the SAME guard**: `put h' warns, then `store_16bit_word' ->
   ONE `memory_rw(len 2)' -> the same arm.  `put w' / `put d' likewise give len 4 / 8.
   **A debugger READ does not reach the arm**: `memory_rw' dispatches a device only if
   `(!no_exceptions || (flags & DM_READS_HAVE_NO_SIDE_EFFECTS))' and pcc2 registers
   `DM_DEFAULT' -- so the six odd blocks' READ-side cells are unreachable by construction.
5. **Arm bodies** (inlined below): VECBASE stores `idata & 0xf0' (+ HUH print); SCCICR
   `& 0x1f'; SCCRX and SCSIICR `& 0xdf'; each of those four then (except VECBASE) calls
   `reassert_interrupts()'.  PSCALEADJ stores nothing, prints "ignored (value ...)".  IPL's
   write body stores nothing and prints "write to read-only PCCTWO_IPL; ignored" once per
   process (#119 static).  SCCERR's write body is `= 0' and nothing in the model stores a
   nonzero SCCERR.  TxCTL `& 3'; TxICR `&= ~0x17 |= idata & 0x17'.  GENCTL and MASK store the
   byte and reassert.  SCCRXIACK has NO width guard and never had one: its body ignores idata.
6. **`fatal()' returns**; the announce is once per block per instance via `d->warned', a
   32-bit field with twelve bits assigned, written ONLY inside `pcc2_width_decline()'.
   `reassert_interrupts()' exits the host only on `GENCTL & 0x04' (pcc2c040kill, filed);
   every GENCTL value in the probe keeps that bit clear.  Every probe session is a fresh
   `pty.fork'/`execv' of the emulator.
7. **The ruling, applied to all twelve now**: clause (d) is a FLOOR; a disclosed asymmetry
   cannot discharge it.  Announce-then-fall-through leaves every `dcl' row and `latch all'
   green by construction, so its only channel is a readback (storing arms) or the arm's own
   print (print-only arms).
8. **Pass-3 roster**: eight answered (codex, kimi, grok, glm, deepseek, minimax, opus, fable5);
   agy DELIBERATELY NOT FIRED (reserved for measurement).  Three seats measured.

## THE CONTRACT (the probe's docstring, VERBATIM, as it now stands)

```
pcc2widthkills DETECTOR -- the twelve width-guard blocks in DEVICE_ACCESS(pcc2).

WHAT THE FIX DID.  Twelve `if (len != 1) { fatal(...); exit(1); }' blocks (ten kills and
two #119 function-static latches) ended the host on a guest-chosen access width.  The
tree's own header says PCC2 registers "can be accessed as bytes, two-bytes, or
four-bytes", so the width is LEGAL.  The fix is a DIRECTION SPLIT:

    READS   ACCEPT -- the memcpy above the switch already served them, raw bytes in
            address order, width-correct by construction.  The guard discarded a
            correct answer.
    WRITES  DECLINE + per-instance latched announce -- every write arm uses `idata',
            the big-endian-composed value, which is "the addressed byte" ONLY at
            len==1.  Deleting the guard would store the NEIGHBOUR's lane.

CONTRACT (vacuity-floor clause (e)) -- what this detector is designed to catch:

  CATCHES
    * reinstatement of the kill at any of the SIX GUEST-reachable blocks (survival
      rows paired with a liveness observable, because exit status cannot tell
      "declined and quit" from "died with status 0");
    * *** pass 2: the two FOLDED-IN arms, IPL and MASK, had NO ROW -- twelve blocks
      converted, ten given rows, and the shipped contract did not say so.  A seat
      found it by sibling parity: MASK is GENCTL's twin and had none of GENCTL's rows.
      Repaired: IPL is EVEN and therefore the SIXTH guest-reachable block (survival +
      message rows); MASK is odd (operator rows) and gets GENCTL's readback. ***
    * reinstatement at any of the six OPERATOR-only blocks (VECBASE, PSCALEADJ, SCCICR,
      SCCRX, SCSIICR, MASK; the T1CTL/T1ICR labels are odd too but share TxCTL/TxICR's
      blocks), through the debugger's `put h' at an odd address -- MEASURED to warn
      and store and kill
      (artifacts/pcc2widthkills/operator_reach_20260903.out);
    * *** DELETE instead of decline on the write side -- the lane hazard.  `st.h
      0xAABB' to GENCTL must leave GENCTL unchanged; a bare deletion stores 0xBB,
      VECBASE's lane, because (uint8_t) idata is the HIGHEST-address byte ***;
    * an accidental per-lane compose (GENCTL would read 0xAA);
    * the value a wide READ composes -- lane order and width -- not merely survival;
    * loss of the latch in either direction: dropped `|=' floods (same site twice
      must announce ONCE), and one bit shared across blocks silences (two sites must
      announce TWICE);
    * the de-escalation clause: byte writes at the LEGAL width at every reachable
      arm still land, read back through each arm's own mask, with NO decline message.

  THE ENUMERATION (pass 3 class closure).  Twelve blocks x six clause-(d) shapes.  Two
  consecutive passes found breaches of ONE class -- a block with no channel for the
  ANNOUNCE-THEN-FALL-THROUGH shape (guard kept, announce kept, `break' removed, the
  halfword LANDS) -- at MASK, then at four odd arms, then (pass 3, MEASURED 82/82 by a
  seat) at VECBASE, whose `rb vecbase' row is GENCTL's neighbour-lane check and never
  writes wide to VECBASE's own arm.  So the space is written down, cell by cell.
  M = measured kill (log named in CHANGELOG/PRETABLE); S = same channel as a measured
  shape at that arm (the mutant lands the write exactly as the measured one does);
  E = equivalent by construction, with its warrant.  Shapes: DEL guard deleted; LEN
  `len != 1' -> `len > 2' (halfword lands, word declined); LEN2 its MIRROR, `len != 1' ->
  `len == 2' (halfword declined, WORD lands -- named by the codex seat on pass 3); KILL
  verbatim exit(1) reinstated; ALIAS one enum member for two blocks; FALL announce-then-
  fall-through; SWAP guard operand `len' -> `idata > 0xff'; GROW the guard widened to
  decline the LEGAL byte width too (`len != 1' -> `len != 0'), the de-escalation clause.

      block      DEL   LEN   LEN2  KILL  ALIAS FALL  SWAP  GROW   FALL channel
      GENCTL     M     S     W     S     C     M     M     A/C    rb genctl (table 1 M_NOBREAK)

 def program(insns, at=CODE):
+    #  `at' lets a session run TWO programs: a second sequence at the SAME address finds
+    #  pc already there and the assignment is skipped (the Addr hazard, designed out in
+    #  the sgi probes with Addr.take()).  Callers needing two pass at=CODE + 0x100.
     words = list(insns) + [I_BR_SELF, 0]
     cmds = ["put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (at + 4 * i, w) for i, w in enumerate(words)]
     return cmds + ["pc=0x%x" % at, "step %d" % len(insns)]

0704: +row("hwr ipl lv", printed(t, "r4") if alive else "died", "0x5a5a5a5a", "...and runs on")
0705: +row("hwr ipl dcl", t.count("write to PCCTWO_IPL " + DECL), 1,
0706: +    "...declined by the WIDTH latch (not the read-only one) -- the message is the channel")
0707: +#  MASK 0x3f: odd, operator path.  It is GENCTL's twin, so it gets GENCTL's readback: set
0708: +#  MASK to a known byte at the legal width, decline a wide write through the debugger,
0709: +#  then read the byte back through a program at a SECOND address.  A deleted guard stores
0710: +#  the wrong lane (0xbb); an accidental compose stores 0xaa.  reassert_interrupts on a
0711: +#  MASK byte write is harmless here: GENCTL's C040 bit is clear and nothing is pending.
0712: +t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x12") + program([ST_B(5, MASK)])
0713: +                        + ["put h 0x%x, 0xaabb" % (PCC2 + MASK)]
0714: +                        + program([LD_BU(4, MASK)], at=CODE + 0x100) + ["print r4"])
0715: +row("op mask", how, "exit0", "debugger put h at odd MASK survives")
0716: +row("op mask dcl", t.count("write to PCCTWO_MASK " + DECL), 1, "...and was declined once")
0717: +row("rb mask", printed(t, "r4") if alive else "died", "0x12",
0718: +    "MASK unchanged after the declined wide write -- GENCTL's twin gets GENCTL's readback")
0719: +
0720:  # ---------------------------------------------------------------------- summary
0721:  print("NOROW  two device instances               -- mvme187 has ONE pcc2; per-instance latch state"
0722:        " is by construction, not measured")
0723: 
0724: ```

0824: ## PRETABLE_amendments.out (Sections 5 and 6)
0825: 
0826: ```
0827: PRETABLE AMENDMENTS -- pcc2widthkills, killtable2_20260903.  Split out of PRETABLE.out on pass 3.
0828: 
0829: 
0830: SECTION 5 -- AMENDMENT, written after table-2 PART 1 (9 mutants + BASE, all killed) and
0831: BEFORE PART 2.  Section 2's "sentence owed" was RULED A FLOOR BREACH, not a disclosure.
0832: --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
0833: The measuring seat, running its own pass 3 against the 78-row detector, asked whether the
0834: disclosed asymmetry (PSCALEADJ/SCCICR/SCCRX/SCSIICR: no readback, so a guard that ANNOUNCES
0835: and then lets the halfword land is invisible) can be discharged by clause (e).  Ruling: NO.
0836: Clause (d) is a FLOOR -- "the guard kept, its effect removed" -- and the fall-through shape at
0837: those arms is exactly that.  Clause (e) governs claims ABOVE the floor; a disclosure cannot
0838: place a detector below it, or every floor breach becomes dischargeable by a sentence.  The
0839: CHANNEL rule in Section 4 already said as much: a `dcl' row cannot vary under a mutant that
0840: keeps announcing, so at those four arms it was the WRONG channel for this shape.
0841: 
0842: Rows added (part 2 measures them):
0843:   rb sccicr / rb sccrx / rb scsiicr   set 0x1f by byte, decline put h 0xaab8, read back 0x1f
0844:                                       M_FALL_* lands 0x18 (& 0x1f) / 0x98 (& 0xdf) and REASSERTS
0845:   op pscale noig                      PSCALEADJ's body stores nothing and only prints; count of
0846:                                       "PSCALEADJ ignored" in the wide-write session == 0.
0847:                                       M_FALL_PSCALEADJ prints it once.
0848: Blast radius is NOT uniform across the four (the seat's point): three of them put the
0849: neighbour's lane into an INTERRUPT CONTROL register and reassert; one prints.  One sentence
0850: had covered all four, which was too coarse as well as below the floor.
0851: 
0852: 
0853: SECTION 6 -- PASS 3: THE CLASS CLOSURE.  Written after pass 3's seats landed and BEFORE
0854: part 3 of the table.
0855: --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
0856: Pass 3 found the SAME class a third time: the fable5 seat MEASURED announce-then-fall-through
0857: at VECBASE as 82/82 (its `pcc2p3_fable/probe_mfall_vecbase.out`).  `rb vecbase' is GENCTL's
0858: neighbour-lane check (st.b VECBASE; st.h GENCTL; ld.bu VECBASE) and Section 2 above credited
0859: it as VECBASE's own readback -- a wrong record that hid the cell.  Independently, glm and grok
0860: named IPL: its write body prints "write to read-only PCCTWO_IPL; ignored" once per process and
0861: stores nothing, so a fall-through has a MESSAGE channel exactly as PSCALEADJ does -- and the
0862: fable5 seat had called that cell a no-op, the same direction the builder was wrong about
0863: PSCALEADJ.  SCCERR's fall-through cell is EQUIVALENT BY CONSTRUCTION: the arm stores 0 and no
0864: code in the model stores a nonzero SCCERR (grep), so decline and land are the same state.
0865: 
0866: The enumeration (12 blocks x 6 shapes) is now written into the probe's CONTRACT with a status
0867: per cell; the two open cells get rows:
0868:   rb vecbase own   st.b 0x50 at VECBASE; put h 0xaab8 at VECBASE; ld.bu -> 0x50.
0869:                    M_FALL_VECBASE lands 0xb0 (0xaab8 & 0xf0) and also prints HUH.
0870:   hwr ipl noig     st.h 0x0303 at IPL (guest-reachable); count of the read-only print == 0.
0871:                    M_FALL_IPL prints it once.
0872: The codex seat added two shapes the six-shape list did not have, both closable at all twelve
0873: blocks by one cardinality row each:
0874:   latch all w      `len != 1' -> `len == 2' (LEN's mirror) declines every halfword row and lets
0875:                    a WORD land; twelve `put w' writes, twelve announces.  M_LEN2_VECBASE -> 11.
0876:   acc all (+lv)    the contract claimed byte writes land "at every reachable arm" while acc
0877:                    rows existed at five blocks.  Twelve legal `put b' writes, ZERO declines.
0878:                    M_GROW_PSCALEADJ (`len != 1' -> `len != 0' at an arm with no acc row) -> 1.
0879: The Opus reader seat MEASURED a ninth shape, 82/82, ONE character: `writeflag == MEM_WRITE'
0880: -> `<= MEM_WRITE', the direction split's own conjunct nulled, so wide READS are declined,
0881: announced as writes, skip the arm's read body and consume the latch bit.  No session holding
0882: a wide read carried a decline-count row.  Closed with ZERO new sessions:
0883:   hrd <block> nod  (six even blocks) and wrd sccerr nod: t.count(DECL) == 0 on the existing
0884:                    wide-read sessions.  M_RDDECL_ALL (all twelve conjuncts) -> seven reds.
0885:   The six odd blocks' read-side cells are UNREACHABLE by construction (guest wide reads at
0886:   odd offsets alignment-fault before memory_rw; debugger reads do not dispatch to DM_DEFAULT
0887:   devices) and are disclosed in the contract.
0888: The T1 labels (deepseek): TxCTL/TxICR are SHARED blocks whose readbacks sat at the T2 offsets;
0889: `rb t1ctl' / `rb t1icr' (operator path, own readbacks) close the T1 half.  M_FALL_TXCTL
0890: (break removed at the shared block) -> rb t2ctl AND rb t1ctl both red.  SCCRXIACK (glm): the
0891: thirteenth arm, never width-guarded, body ignores idata -- named in the contract as excluded.
0892: Part 3 measures BASE 96/96, M_FALL_VECBASE, M_FALL_IPL, M_LEN2_VECBASE, M_GROW_PSCALEADJ,
0893: M_RDDECL_ALL, M_FALL_TXCTL.  Every other cell keeps its measured or same-channel status from
0894: parts 1-2 and the builder's pass 3/3b/3c: adding rows cannot un-kill a mutant.  A closing
0895: pass 4 verifies THIS enumeration (12 x 9 plus the two T1 labels, written into the contract);
0896: it does not hunt cells.
0897: 
0898: 
0899: SECTION 7 -- PART 3 MEASURED (killtable2_20260903/part3/, part3b/), and what it corrected.
0900: --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
0901: Part 3 (96-row instrument): BASE 95/96 -- `rb t1ctl' red on the fix by a STRING mismatch (the
0902: row wanted "0x03", the debugger prints "0x3"; the value was right).  Fixed; part 3b BASE 96/96.
0903: Kills, each on exactly the row Section 6 named (the artifact row subtracted):
0904:   M_FALL_VECBASE    rb vecbase own          M_FALL_IPL       hwr ipl noig
0905:   M_LEN2_VECBASE    latch all w             M_RDDECL_ALL     all seven hrd/wrd * nod (88/96)
0906:   M_FALL_TXCTL      rb t2ctl AND rb t1ctl   (part 3b, BASE full: the T1 cell measured)
0907: ESCAPE: M_GROW_PSCALEADJ 95/96 (part 3) and 96/96 (part 3b) -- `acc all' did not move, WITH
0908: the mutation PROVEN in the source (1 x `len != 0') and the binary newer than the source (3b's
0909: proof lines).  So the row could not see the arm: the debugger's `put b' calls memory_rw with
0910: NO_EXCEPTIONS, and memory_rw.c's device dispatch is `if (!no_exceptions || (flags &
0911: DM_READS_HAVE_NO_SIDE_EFFECTS))'; pcc2 registers DM_DEFAULT, so a NO_EXCEPTIONS byte write is
0912: never dispatched.  `put h/w/d' go through store_Nbit_word and do reach the arms (every other
0913: cardinality row worked).  THE ROW WAS VACUOUS BY CONSTRUCTION -- the CHANNEL rule's failure
0914: condition, caught by the table rather than at writing time.  `acc all' is now twelve GUEST
0915: `st.b' stores (legal at every offset), and part 3c re-measures BASE + M_GROW_PSCALEADJ.
0916: The contract now states the `put b' fact so no future row rests on it.

#ifndef MVME_PCCTWOREG_H
#define MVME_PCCTWOREG_H

/*
 * Memory map for PCC2 chip found in MVME1x7 boards.
 *
 * PCCchip2 control and status register can be accessed as bytes (8 bits),
 * two-bytes (16 bits), or four-bytes (32 bits).
 */

#define	PCC2_BASE		0xfff42000
#define PCC2_SIZE		0x0040

#define	PCCTWO_CHIPID		0x0000
#define	PCCTWO_CHIPREV		0x0001
#define	PCCTWO_GENCTL		0x0002
#define	PCCTWO_VECBASE		0x0003
#define	PCCTWO_T1CMP		0x0004
#define	PCCTWO_T1COUNT		0x0008
#define	PCCTWO_T2CMP		0x000c
#define	PCCTWO_T2COUNT		0x0010
#define	PCCTWO_PSCALECNT	0x0014
#define	PCCTWO_PSCALEADJ	0x0015
#define	PCCTWO_T2CTL		0x0016
#define	PCCTWO_T1CTL		0x0017
#define	PCCTWO_GPIO_ICR		0x0018
#define	PCCTWO_GPIO_PCR		0x0019
#define	PCCTWO_T2ICR		0x001a
#define	PCCTWO_T1ICR		0x001b
#define	PCCTWO_SCCERR		0x001c
#define	PCCTWO_SCCICR		0x001d
#define	PCCTWO_SCCTX		0x001e
#define	PCCTWO_SCCRX		0x001f
#define	PCCTWO_SCCMOIACK	0x0023
#define	PCCTWO_SCCTXIACK	0x0025
#define	PCCTWO_SCCRXIACK	0x0027
#define	PCCTWO_IEERR		0x0028
#define	PCCTWO_IEICR		0x002a
#define	PCCTWO_IEBERR		0x002b
#define	PCCTWO_SCSIERR		0x002c
#define	PCCTWO_SCSIICR		0x002f
#define	PCCTWO_PRTICR		0x0030
#define	PCCTWO_PTRFICR		0x0031
#define	PCCTWO_PTRSICR		0x0032
#define	PCCTWO_PTRPICR		0x0033
#define	PCCTWO_PRTBICR		0x0034
#define	PCCTWO_PRTSTATUS	0x0036
#define	PCCTWO_PRTCTL		0x0037
#define	PCCTWO_SPEED		0x0038
#define	PCCTWO_PRTDATA		0x003a
/* The following registers are not valid on MVME197 */
#define	PCCTWO_IPL		0x003e
#define	PCCTWO_MASK		0x003f

#define PCC2_ID			0x20	/* value at CHIPID */

/* General Control Register */
#define PCC2_DR0		0x80
#define PCC2_C040		0x04
#define PCC2_MIEN		0x02
#define PCC2_FAST		0x01

/* Top 4 bits of the PCC2 VBR. Will be the top 4 bits of the vector */
#define	PCC2_VECT		0x50

/* Bottom 4 bits of the vector returned during IACK cycle */
#define PCC2V_PPBUSY		0x00 				/* lowest */
#define PCC2V_PPPE		0x01
#define PCC2V_PPSELECT		0x02
#define PCC2V_PPFAULT		0x03
#define PCC2V_PPACK		0x04
#define PCC2V_SCSI		0x05
#define PCC2V_IEFAIL		0x06
#define PCC2V_IE		0x07
#define PCC2V_TIMER2		0x08
#define PCC2V_TIMER1		0x09
#define PCC2V_GPIO		0x0a
#define PCC2V_SCC_RXE		0x0c
#define PCC2V_SCC_M		(PCC2V_SCC_RXE + 1)
#define PCC2V_SCC_TX		(PCC2V_SCC_M + 1)
#define PCC2V_SCC_RX		(PCC2V_SCC_TX + 1)

/*
 * We lock off our interrupt vector at 0x50.
 */
#define PCC2_VECBASE		0x50
#define PCC2_NVEC		0x10

#define PCC2_TCTL_CEN		0x01
#define PCC2_TCTL_COC		0x02
#define PCC2_TCTL_COVF		0x04
#define PCC2_TCTL_OVF		0xf0

#define PCC2_GPIO_PLTY		0x80
#define PCC2_GPIO_EL		0x40

#define PCC2_GPIOCR_OE		0x2
#define PCC2_GPIOCR_O		0x1

#define PCC2_SCC_AVEC		0x08

#define PCC2_SC_INHIBIT		(0 << 6)
#define PCC2_SC_SNOOP		(1 << 6)
#define PCC2_SC_INVAL		(2 << 6)
#define PCC2_SC_RESV		(3 << 6)

#define pcc2_timer_us2lim(us)	(us)		/* timer increments in "us" */

#define PCC2_IRQ_IPL		0x07
#define PCC2_IRQ_ICLR		0x08
#define PCC2_IRQ_IEN		0x10
#define PCC2_IRQ_INT		0x20

/* Tick Timer Interrupt Control Register */
#define PCC2_TTIRQ_INT		0x20

#define PCC2_GENCTL_FAST	0x01
#define PCC2_GENCTL_IEN		0x02
#define PCC2_GENCTL_C040	0x03

#endif	/*  MVME_PCCTWOREG_H  */

*** SECTION 0 -- THE HONEST PART: THIS ROUND'S TABLE 1 (killtable_20260903, 8 mutants, 60
*** rows) RAN WITH NO PRE-TABLE BLOCK.  The pass-2 measuring seat named the omission and
*** measured what the skipped SIBLING-PARITY check would have shown: GENCTL and MASK have
*** byte-identical write bodies, GENCTL carried five row mentions and MASK zero, and the
*** MASK null-repair was a 60/60 sweep.  The class -- aliasing a latch constant, one token,
*** clause (d) -- sat in sgideprimop's own PRETABLE.out 33 hours earlier, and this round
*** transferred that file's d->warned design without the check that qualified it.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
This block was written BEFORE table 2 part 1 (13:07:27 by the checkpoint and CHANGELOG
records; part 1's driver.log starts 13:07:43).  ITS MTIME NO LONGER PROVES THAT: Section 5
was appended into this file at 13:56, after part 1 had finished, which a pass-3 seat
caught.  Amendments now live in PRETABLE_amendments.out beside this file; this file
carries Sections 0-4 as first written, with one bracketed annotation in Section 2.  Table 1 is recorded as HAVING HAD NO
PRE-TABLE BLOCK, not as having passed one.  (Third recorded skip of the mandate.)

SECTION 1 -- GUARD FIELD vs BODY FIELD
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
GUARD reads (each of 12 blocks):  len, writeflag
DECLINE body reads:               d->warned (bit test), len (printed)     writes: d->warned |= bit
FALL-THROUGH body reads:          idata, relative_addr, d->pcctwo_reg[], pending_timer*_interrupts

Where they differ: the guard reads the ACCESS SHAPE, the body reads the VALUE.
Disagreeing states: (len==1, any idata)      -> acc rows: byte writes land
                    (len==2, idata > 0xff)   -> every hwr/rb/wwr/latch row
                    (len==2, idata <= 0xff)  -> NO ROW EXISTED.  All 77 wide-write rows used
                    values above 0xff, so a guard operand swap `len != 1' -> `idata > 0xff'
                    (a plausible "fits a byte" reading) was green by construction while a
                    big-endian st.h 0x0022 stored 0x22 -- the neighbour's lane -- into GENCTL.
  ==> ADDED before the table: `rb genctl lo' (st.b 0x11; st.h 0x0022; expect 0x11) and the
      `latch all' value changed 0x0303 -> 0x0003 so the cardinality row reds under the same
      swap at ANY arm.  Mutant M_IDATA_GENCTL built to measure it.  This is the block's own
      yield, found by asking the field question rather than the mutant question.

THE SHARED-BITFIELD QUESTION (who else writes d->warned, can they set my bit?):
    occurrences of 'warned |=' in dev_pcc2.c: 1      lines mentioning 'warned': 7
  One writer, pcc2_width_decline(), sets exactly the bit its caller names; nothing in
  DEVICE_ACCESS or a tick writes the field, so benign traffic cannot pre-set a bit.  What
  CAN collide is the ENUM: two callers naming one member.  That is the M_ALIAS_SCCTX shape,
  and `latch all' (12 announces, one per block) is the row that sees it -- `latch same' and
  `latch two' pin one pair of 66 and do not.

SECTION 2 -- SIBLING PARITY, ARM BY ARM (after the repair; every block owes a survival row,
a decline-announce row, and a readback where decline != accept)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  block      even?  survival            announce                  readback (decline != accept?)
  GENCTL     even   hwr genctl(+lv)     rb genctl dcl             rb genctl, val h genctl, rb genctl lo, acc genctl
  VECBASE    odd    op vecbase          op vecbase dcl            rb vecbase
  PSCALEADJ  odd    op pscale           op pscale dcl             plain store, no mask: dcl is the channel
  TxCTL      even   hwr t2ctl(+lv)      rb t2ctl dcl              rb t2ctl (& 3), acc t2ctl, op t1ctl(+dcl)
  TxICR      even   hwr t2icr(+lv)      rb t2icr dcl              rb t2icr (& 0x17), acc t2icr, op t1icr(+dcl)
  SCCERR     even   hrd/hwr/wrd/wwr     hwr sccerr dcl, wwr dcl   VALUE-IDENTICAL (always 0): message only, disclosed
  SCCICR     odd    op sccicr           op sccicr dcl             dcl is the channel
  SCCTX      even   hwr scctx(+lv)      rb scctx dcl              rb scctx (& 0x1f), acc scctx
  SCCRX      odd    op sccrx            op sccrx dcl              dcl is the channel
  SCSIICR    odd    op scsiicr          op scsiicr dcl            dcl is the channel
  IPL        even   hrd/hwr ipl(+lv)    hwr ipl dcl               read-side IPL==0 diagnostic (width-independent)
  MASK       odd    op mask             op mask dcl               rb mask (GENCTL's twin: plain store + reassert)
  latch all  (all twelve, one offset per BLOCK -> 12 announces)
Asymmetry left, DISCLOSED: PSCALEADJ/SCCICR/SCCRX/SCSIICR have no readback row.
  [Pass 3 annotation: this table WRONGLY credits `rb vecbase' as VECBASE's readback; it is
   GENCTL's neighbour-lane row.  VECBASE belonged in this list, and IPL's 'width-independent'
   parenthesis was the CHANNEL rule's own failure condition written down and not carried.]  Their `dcl'
rows red under any guard edit that lets the halfword land silently (len > 2, delete, operand
swap) -- every shape in this table.  A guard edit that lands the write AND still announces
(the M_FALL_MASK shape) at one of those four would escape; that is the sentence owed, and
MASK's own fall-through is the measured instance of the shape.

SECTION 4 -- CHANNEL: mutant -> row -> observable that differs.  PREDICTIONS, written
before any table-2 cell landed.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  M_DELETE_MASK    rb mask 0xbb (was 0x0), op mask dcl 0            MASK guard deleted: halfword lands the neighbour lane
  M_KILL_IPL       hwr ipl exit1, hwr ipl lv died                  verbatim kill at a GUEST-reachable arm
  M_KILL_MASK      op mask exit1                                    kill at the operator-reachable twin
  M_DELETE (ctl)   rb genctl 0xbb, rb genctl dcl 0                  the table-1 control still reds

  M_LENGT2_TXICR   same                                             per-arm `len > 2'

  M_LENGT2_SCCTX   rb scctx 0x18, rb scctx dcl 0                    0xaab8 & 0x1f lands
  M_LENGT2_SCCERR  hwr sccerr dcl 0                                 value-identical: the announce is the only channel
  M_ALIAS_SCCTX    latch all 11 (SCCTX announces on SCCRX's bit)    a collision drops the count; nothing else moves
  M_LENGT2_MASK    rb mask 0xbb, op mask dcl 0                      halfword lands at MASK
  M_FALL_MASK      rb mask 0xbb (op mask dcl stays 1)               announce-then-land: readback is the only channel
  M_IDATA_GENCTL   rb genctl lo 0x22, latch all 11                  operand swap to a value test: a small halfword lands
  BASE             78/78                                            the repair perturbed nothing
Every row above names a mutant under which its channel VARIES; no row is survival-only at a
refusal-effect site.  Rows whose channel cannot vary under any enumerated mutant: none added.


PRETABLE AMENDMENTS -- pcc2widthkills, killtable2_20260903.  Split out of PRETABLE.out on pass 3.


SECTION 5 -- AMENDMENT, written after table-2 PART 1 (9 mutants + BASE, all killed) and
BEFORE PART 2.  Section 2's "sentence owed" was RULED A FLOOR BREACH, not a disclosure.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
The measuring seat, running its own pass 3 against the 78-row detector, asked whether the
disclosed asymmetry (PSCALEADJ/SCCICR/SCCRX/SCSIICR: no readback, so a guard that ANNOUNCES
and then lets the halfword land is invisible) can be discharged by clause (e).  Ruling: NO.
Clause (d) is a FLOOR -- "the guard kept, its effect removed" -- and the fall-through shape at
those arms is exactly that.  Clause (e) governs claims ABOVE the floor; a disclosure cannot
place a detector below it, or every floor breach becomes dischargeable by a sentence.  The
CHANNEL rule in Section 4 already said as much: a `dcl' row cannot vary under a mutant that
keeps announcing, so at those four arms it was the WRONG channel for this shape.

Rows added (part 2 measures them):
  rb sccicr / rb sccrx / rb scsiicr   set 0x1f by byte, decline put h 0xaab8, read back 0x1f
                                      M_FALL_* lands 0x18 (& 0x1f) / 0x98 (& 0xdf) and REASSERTS
  op pscale noig                      PSCALEADJ's body stores nothing and only prints; count of
                                      "PSCALEADJ ignored" in the wide-write session == 0.
                                      M_FALL_PSCALEADJ prints it once.
Blast radius is NOT uniform across the four (the seat's point): three of them put the
neighbour's lane into an INTERRUPT CONTROL register and reassert; one prints.  One sentence
had covered all four, which was too coarse as well as below the floor.


SECTION 6 -- PASS 3: THE CLASS CLOSURE.  Written after pass 3's seats landed and BEFORE
part 3 of the table.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Pass 3 found the SAME class a third time: the fable5 seat MEASURED announce-then-fall-through
at VECBASE as 82/82 (its `pcc2p3_fable/probe_mfall_vecbase.out`).  `rb vecbase' is GENCTL's
neighbour-lane check (st.b VECBASE; st.h GENCTL; ld.bu VECBASE) and Section 2 above credited
it as VECBASE's own readback -- a wrong record that hid the cell.  Independently, glm and grok
named IPL: its write body prints "write to read-only PCCTWO_IPL; ignored" once per process and
stores nothing, so a fall-through has a MESSAGE channel exactly as PSCALEADJ does -- and the
fable5 seat had called that cell a no-op, the same direction the builder was wrong about
PSCALEADJ.  SCCERR's fall-through cell is EQUIVALENT BY CONSTRUCTION: the arm stores 0 and no
code in the model stores a nonzero SCCERR (grep), so decline and land are the same state.

The enumeration (12 blocks x 6 shapes) is now written into the probe's CONTRACT with a status
per cell; the two open cells get rows:
  rb vecbase own   st.b 0x50 at VECBASE; put h 0xaab8 at VECBASE; ld.bu -> 0x50.
                   M_FALL_VECBASE lands 0xb0 (0xaab8 & 0xf0) and also prints HUH.
  hwr ipl noig     st.h 0x0303 at IPL (guest-reachable); count of the read-only print == 0.
                   M_FALL_IPL prints it once.
The codex seat added two shapes the six-shape list did not have, both closable at all twelve
blocks by one cardinality row each:
  latch all w      `len != 1' -> `len == 2' (LEN's mirror) declines every halfword row and lets
                   a WORD land; twelve `put w' writes, twelve announces.  M_LEN2_VECBASE -> 11.
  acc all (+lv)    the contract claimed byte writes land "at every reachable arm" while acc
                   rows existed at five blocks.  Twelve legal `put b' writes, ZERO declines.
                   M_GROW_PSCALEADJ (`len != 1' -> `len != 0' at an arm with no acc row) -> 1.
The Opus reader seat MEASURED a ninth shape, 82/82, ONE character: `writeflag == MEM_WRITE'
-> `<= MEM_WRITE', the direction split's own conjunct nulled, so wide READS are declined,
announced as writes, skip the arm's read body and consume the latch bit.  No session holding
a wide read carried a decline-count row.  Closed with ZERO new sessions:
  hrd <block> nod  (six even blocks) and wrd sccerr nod: t.count(DECL) == 0 on the existing
                   wide-read sessions.  M_RDDECL_ALL (all twelve conjuncts) -> seven reds.
  The six odd blocks' read-side cells are UNREACHABLE by construction (guest wide reads at
  odd offsets alignment-fault before memory_rw; debugger reads do not dispatch to DM_DEFAULT
  devices) and are disclosed in the contract.
The T1 labels (deepseek): TxCTL/TxICR are SHARED blocks whose readbacks sat at the T2 offsets;
`rb t1ctl' / `rb t1icr' (operator path, own readbacks) close the T1 half.  M_FALL_TXCTL
(break removed at the shared block) -> rb t2ctl AND rb t1ctl both red.  SCCRXIACK (glm): the
thirteenth arm, never width-guarded, body ignores idata -- named in the contract as excluded.
Part 3 measures BASE 96/96, M_FALL_VECBASE, M_FALL_IPL, M_LEN2_VECBASE, M_GROW_PSCALEADJ,
M_RDDECL_ALL, M_FALL_TXCTL.  Every other cell keeps its measured or same-channel status from
parts 1-2 and the builder's pass 3/3b/3c: adding rows cannot un-kill a mutant.  A closing
pass 4 verifies THIS enumeration (12 x 9 plus the two T1 labels, written into the contract);
it does not hunt cells.


SECTION 7 -- PART 3 MEASURED (killtable2_20260903/part3/, part3b/), and what it corrected.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Part 3 (96-row instrument): BASE 95/96 -- `rb t1ctl' red on the fix by a STRING mismatch (the
row wanted "0x03", the debugger prints "0x3"; the value was right).  Fixed; part 3b BASE 96/96.
Kills, each on exactly the row Section 6 named (the artifact row subtracted):
  M_FALL_VECBASE    rb vecbase own          M_FALL_IPL       hwr ipl noig
  M_LEN2_VECBASE    latch all w             M_RDDECL_ALL     all seven hrd/wrd * nod (88/96)
  M_FALL_TXCTL      rb t2ctl AND rb t1ctl   (part 3b, BASE full: the T1 cell measured)
ESCAPE: M_GROW_PSCALEADJ 95/96 (part 3) and 96/96 (part 3b) -- `acc all' did not move, WITH
the mutation PROVEN in the source (1 x `len != 0') and the binary newer than the source (3b's
proof lines).  So the row could not see the arm: the debugger's `put b' calls memory_rw with
NO_EXCEPTIONS, and memory_rw.c's device dispatch is `if (!no_exceptions || (flags &
DM_READS_HAVE_NO_SIDE_EFFECTS))'; pcc2 registers DM_DEFAULT, so a NO_EXCEPTIONS byte write is
never dispatched.  `put h/w/d' go through store_Nbit_word and do reach the arms (every other
cardinality row worked).  THE ROW WAS VACUOUS BY CONSTRUCTION -- the CHANNEL rule's failure
condition, caught by the table rather than at writing time.  `acc all' is now twelve GUEST
`st.b' stores (legal at every offset), and part 3c re-measures BASE + M_GROW_PSCALEADJ.
The contract now states the `put b' fact so no future row rests on it.


codex
A concrete escape has emerged in the latch update, outside the nine listed shapes. I’m now checking every probe session to ensure it really remains observationally equivalent across all 96 rows, and separately validating the table’s S/E/unreachable claims.

0374: #  the 12-bit space drops the count.  (Operator path for all twelve, so the odd ones are
0375: #  reachable; the guest-reachable halves have their own rows above.)
0376: BLOCKS12 = (("GENCTL", 0x02), ("VECBASE", 0x03), ("PSCALEADJ", 0x15), ("TxCTL", 0x16),
0377:             ("TxICR", 0x1a), ("SCCERR", 0x1c), ("SCCICR", 0x1d), ("SCCTX", 0x1e),
0378:             ("SCCRX", 0x1f), ("SCSIICR", 0x2f), ("IPL", 0x3e), ("MASK", 0x3f))
0379: #  VALUE 0x0003, not 0x0303: it fits a byte, so this row ALSO reds if any arm swaps its
0380: #  guard operand from len to a value test (see `rb genctl lo').  On the fix the value
0381: #  never lands, so the expectation is value-independent.
0382: t, alive, how = session(["put h 0x%x, 0x0003" % (PCC2 + o) for _, o in BLOCKS12])
0383: row("latch all", t.count(DECL), 12,
0384:     "one wide write per BLOCK -> TWELVE announces: bit CARDINALITY, not one pair")
0385: row("latch all lv", how, "exit0", "...and the host survived all twelve blocks")
0386: 
0387: # ------------------------------------------------------------------ latch rows
0388: t, alive, how = session(setup(r6="0xaabb") + program([ST_H(6, GENCTL), ST_H(6, GENCTL)]))
0389: row("latch same", t.count("write to PCCTWO_GENCTL " + DECL), 1,
0390:     "same block twice -> ONE announce -- kills a dropped |= (flood)")
0391: t, alive, how = session(setup(r6="0x0303") + program([ST_H(6, GENCTL), ST_H(6, T2CTL)]))
0392: row("latch two", t.count(DECL), 2,
0393:     "two blocks -> TWO announces -- kills a bit shared across blocks")
0394: 
0395: # --------------------------------------------- de-escalation ACCEPT rows (byte writes)
0396: #  Removing twelve exit(1)s destroys the tripwire that announced guard growth.  So the
0397: #  legal width must still LAND at each reachable arm, through its own mask, with no
0398: #  decline message.  Expected values from the arms' own semantics.
0399: for nm, off, wr, rdv in (("genctl", GENCTL, "0x82", "0x82"),     # stores the byte; bit 0x04 clear
0400:                           ("t2ctl", T2CTL, "0x03", "0x3"),       # &= 0xfc; |= (v & 3)
0401:                           ("t2icr", T2ICR, "0x13", "0x13"),      # &= ~0x17; |= (v & 0x17)
0402:                           ("scctx", SCCTX, "0x1f", "0x1f"),      # = v & 0x1f
0403:                           ("sccerr", SCCERR, "0x5a", "0x0")):    # any write clears to 0
0404:     t, alive, how = session(setup(r5=wr) + program([ST_B(5, off), LD_BU(4, off)]) + ["print r4"])
0405:     row("acc " + nm, printed(t, "r4") if alive else "died", rdv,
0406:         "st.b %s at the legal width lands through its own mask" % nm.upper())
0407:     row("acc %s nod" % nm, t.count(DECL), 0, "...with NO decline message")
0408: 
0409: # ------------------------------- OPERATOR path: the seven odd blocks via `put h'
0410: #  The m88k alignment check makes these guest-unreachable; the debugger warns on a
0411: #  misaligned put and stores anyway, straight to the handler (MEASURED pre-fix: exit1).
0412: for nm, off in (("vecbase", VECBASE), ("pscale", PSCALEADJ), ("t1ctl", T1CTL),
0413:                 ("t1icr", T1ICR), ("sccicr", SCCICR), ("sccrx", SCCRX), ("scsiicr", SCSIICR)):
0414:     t, alive, how = session(["put h 0x%x, 0x0303" % (PCC2 + off)])
0415:     row("op " + nm, how, "exit0", "debugger put h at odd %s survives" % nm.upper())
0416:     row("op %s dcl" % nm, t.count(DECL), 1, "...and was declined once")
0417: 
0418: #  --------- the odd arms' FALL-THROUGH channel (pass 3, ruled a floor breach, not a disclosure)
0419: #  The block above proves each odd arm ANNOUNCES; it cannot see a guard that announces AND
0420: #  then lets the halfword land (the M_FALL shape -- decline nulled, guard kept: clause (d)).
0421: #  PRETABLE Section 2 had disclosed that as "a sentence owed".  The measuring seat asked
0422: #  whether a disclosure can discharge a floor clause; it cannot, or every breach becomes a
0423: #  sentence.  So each of the four arms gets the channel its body offers:
0424: #    SCCICR/SCCRX/SCSIICR store `idata & mask' then reassert_interrupts(): set 0x1f by
0425: #      byte (legal under both masks), decline a wide 0xaab8, read the byte back -- a landed
0426: #      write leaves 0x18 (& 0x1f) or 0x98 (& 0xdf) in an INTERRUPT CONTROL register.
0427: #    PSCALEADJ stores nothing; its write body only prints "ignored (value ...)".  A landed
0428: #      wide write therefore prints that line -- the message is the only channel, and the
0429: #      row counts it at ZERO.
0430: for nm, off in (("sccicr", SCCICR), ("sccrx", SCCRX), ("scsiicr", SCSIICR)):
0431:     t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x1f") + program([ST_B(5, off)])
0432:                             + ["put h 0x%x, 0xaab8" % (PCC2 + off)]
0433:                             + program([LD_BU(4, off)], at=CODE + 0x100) + ["print r4"])
0434:     row("rb " + nm, printed(t, "r4") if alive else "died", "0x1f",
0435:         "%s unchanged after a declined put h -- a fall-through lands 0x18/0x98 and reasserts" % nm.upper())
0436: t, alive, how = session(["put h 0x%x, 0x0303" % (PCC2 + PSCALEADJ)])
0437: row("op pscale noig", t.count("PSCALEADJ ignored"), 0,
0438:     "a declined wide write never reaches the 'ignored' print -- fall-through would")
0439: 
0440: #  PASS 3 (class closure).  `rb vecbase' above writes wide to GENCTL and reads VECBASE: it
0441: #  is GENCTL's neighbour-lane check and never exercises VECBASE's own guard.  A seat
0442: #  MEASURED announce-then-fall-through at VECBASE as 82/82 -- the halfword landed (& 0xf0)
0443: #  while `op vecbase dcl' and `latch all' stayed green by construction.  VECBASE is the
0444: #  interrupt vector base, so the landed lane mis-delivers every vector.  Its own readback:
0445: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x50") + program([ST_B(5, VECBASE)])
0446:                         + ["put h 0x%x, 0xaab8" % (PCC2 + VECBASE)]
0447:                         + program([LD_BU(4, VECBASE)], at=CODE + 0x100) + ["print r4"])
0448: row("rb vecbase own", printed(t, "r4") if alive else "died", "0x50",
0449:     "VECBASE unchanged after a declined put h to ITS OWN arm -- a fall-through lands 0xb0")
0450: #  IPL's write body stores nothing and prints "write to read-only PCCTWO_IPL; ignored"
0451: #  once per process (#119 static).  A declined wide write never reaches it; a fall-through
0452: #  prints it -- PSCALEADJ's shape at the twelfth block.  The message is the channel.
0453: t, alive, how = session(setup(r6="0x0303") + program([ST_H(6, IPL)]))
0454: row("hwr ipl noig", t.count("write to read-only PCCTWO_IPL"), 0,
0455:     "a declined st.h to IPL never reaches the read-only print -- fall-through would")
0456: #  The codex seat, pass 3: the MIRROR of the measured width edit.  `len != 1' -> `len == 2'
0457: #  declines every halfword row here and lets a WORD write land -- every row above stayed
0458: #  green under it.  One cardinality row over WORD writes closes that at all twelve blocks.
0459: #  (Word writes to the counter arms are pcc2counter8's business and are not touched.)
0460: t, alive, how = session(["put w 0x%x, 0x00000003" % (PCC2 + o) for _, o in BLOCKS12])
0461: row("latch all w", t.count(DECL), 12,
0462:     "one WORD write per BLOCK -> TWELVE announces: a `len == 2' guard anywhere drops it")
0463: #  And the de-escalation side, closed at all twelve: one LEGAL byte write per block must
0464: #  announce NOTHING.  A guard grown to refuse bytes (`len != 1' -> `len != 0') at any of
0465: #  the seven blocks without a per-arm `acc' row is visible only here.  Values: GENCTL keeps
0466: #  bit 0x04 clear (pcc2c040kill); VECBASE's mask is 0xf0; the rest accept any byte.
0467: #  *** MEASURED, part 3 and 3b: this row's first form used the debugger's `put b' and could
0468: #  NOT SEE a guard grown to refuse bytes at PSCALEADJ -- 96/96 with the mutation PROVEN in the
0469: #  source and the binary newer than it.  `put b' calls memory_rw directly with NO_EXCEPTIONS,
0470: #  and that flag does not dispatch to a DM_DEFAULT device; `put h/w/d' go through the
0471: #  store_Nbit_word helpers and do reach the arms.  So the operator path for BYTES is not a
0472: #  path at all, and the row was vacuous by construction -- the CHANNEL rule's own failure,
0473: #  caught by the table.  Guest `st.b' is legal at every offset (byte accesses skip the m88k
0474: #  alignment check; `rb mask' stores a byte at 0x3f this way), so the accept side is pinned
0475: #  from the GUEST at all twelve blocks, which is the stronger form anyway. ***
0476: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x10") + program([ST_B(5, o) for _, o in BLOCKS12]))
0477: row("acc all", t.count(DECL), 0,
0478:     "twelve legal guest byte stores, ZERO declines -- the accept side at every block")
0479: row("acc all lv", how, "exit0", "...and the host survived all twelve byte stores")
0480: #  The T1 labels of the two SHARED blocks (deepseek, pass 3): their readbacks above are at
0481: #  the T2 offsets, so a fall-through reaching only the T1 half of a split block would leave
0482: #  `op t1ctl dcl' at 1 with nothing reading T1CTL back.  Operator path, own readbacks:
0483: #  (`want' is the value as the debugger PRINTS it -- 0x03 comes back as `0x3'.  The first
0484: #  part-3 BASE run reddened this row on the fix for exactly that string mismatch, 95/96;
0485: #  the value was right.  Re-measured in part 3b.)
0486: for nm, off, pre, want in (("t1ctl", T1CTL, "0x03", "0x3"), ("t1icr", T1ICR, "0x13", "0x13")):
0487:     t, alive, how = session(setup(r5=pre) + program([ST_B(5, off)])
0488:                             + ["put h 0x%x, 0xaab8" % (PCC2 + off)]
0489:                             + program([LD_BU(4, off)], at=CODE + 0x100) + ["print r4"])
0490:     row("rb " + nm, printed(t, "r4") if alive else "died", want,
0491:         "%s unchanged after a declined put h at the T1 label -- a fall-through lands 0xb8 & mask" % nm.upper())
0492: 
0493: # --------------------------------- IPL (guest-reachable, EVEN) and MASK (operator, odd)
0494: #  IPL 0x3e: a halfword at 0x3e lands in the IPL arm.  Its write side is read-only (#119
0495: #  "ignored"), so a wide write is declined by the WIDTH latch first and a readback cannot
0496: #  tell the two refusals apart -- the channel is the message.
0497: t, alive, how = session(setup() + program([I_ST_SCR, LD_H(4, IPL), I_LD_SCR]) + ["print r4"])
0498: row("hrd ipl", how, "exit0", "ld.h IPL (EVEN, the SIXTH guest-reachable block) survives")
0499: row("hrd ipl lv", printed(t, "r4") if alive else "died", "0x5a5a5a5a", "...and runs on")
0500: row("hrd ipl nod", t.count(DECL), 0, "...and the wide READ of IPL is never declined")
0501: t, alive, how = session(setup(r6="0x1234") + program([I_ST_SCR, ST_H(6, IPL), I_LD_SCR]) + ["print r4"])
0502: row("hwr ipl", how, "exit0", "st.h IPL survives")
0503: row("hwr ipl lv", printed(t, "r4") if alive else "died", "0x5a5a5a5a", "...and runs on")
0504: row("hwr ipl dcl", t.count("write to PCCTWO_IPL " + DECL), 1,
0505:     "...declined by the WIDTH latch (not the read-only one) -- the message is the channel")
0506: #  MASK 0x3f: odd, operator path.  It is GENCTL's twin, so it gets GENCTL's readback: set
0507: #  MASK to a known byte at the legal width, decline a wide write through the debugger,
0508: #  then read the byte back through a program at a SECOND address.  A deleted guard stores
0509: #  the wrong lane (0xbb); an accidental compose stores 0xaa.  reassert_interrupts on a
0510: #  MASK byte write is harmless here: GENCTL's C040 bit is clear and nothing is pending.
0511: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x12") + program([ST_B(5, MASK)])
0512:                         + ["put h 0x%x, 0xaabb" % (PCC2 + MASK)]
0513:                         + program([LD_BU(4, MASK)], at=CODE + 0x100) + ["print r4"])
0514: row("op mask", how, "exit0", "debugger put h at odd MASK survives")
0515: row("op mask dcl", t.count("write to PCCTWO_MASK " + DECL), 1, "...and was declined once")
0516: row("rb mask", printed(t, "r4") if alive else "died", "0x12",
0517:     "MASK unchanged after the declined wide write -- GENCTL's twin gets GENCTL's readback")
0518: 
0519: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------- summary
0520: print("NOROW  two device instances               -- mvme187 has ONE pcc2; per-instance latch state"
0521:       " is by construction, not measured")
0522: print("NOROW  pcc2c040kill                        -- a LEGAL byte write of 0x04 to GENCTL still ends"
0523:       " the host; every GENCTL lane here keeps that bit clear")
0524: print("NOROW  pcc2defaultkill / pcc2counter8      -- other arms, other rounds")

 def program(insns, at=CODE):
+    #  `at' lets a session run TWO programs: a second sequence at the SAME address finds
+    #  pc already there and the assignment is skipped (the Addr hazard, designed out in
+    #  the sgi probes with Addr.take()).  Callers needing two pass at=CODE + 0x100.
     words = list(insns) + [I_BR_SELF, 0]
     cmds = ["put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (at + 4 * i, w) for i, w in enumerate(words)]
     return cmds + ["pc=0x%x" % at, "step %d" % len(insns)]

+#  and TxICR serves T1ICR+T2ICR, so twelve LABELS would give only ten announces -- the
+#  same units slip that let "five even blocks + seven odd ones = twelve" read as all
+#  twelve blocks.  This row pins the CARDINALITY of the latch: any collision anywhere in
+#  the 12-bit space drops the count.  (Operator path for all twelve, so the odd ones are
+#  reachable; the guest-reachable halves have their own rows above.)
+BLOCKS12 = (("GENCTL", 0x02), ("VECBASE", 0x03), ("PSCALEADJ", 0x15), ("TxCTL", 0x16),
