+            ("SCCRX", 0x1f), ("SCSIICR", 0x2f), ("IPL", 0x3e), ("MASK", 0x3f))
+#  VALUE 0x0003, not 0x0303: it fits a byte, so this row ALSO reds if any arm swaps its
+#  guard operand from len to a value test (see `rb genctl lo').  On the fix the value
+#  never lands, so the expectation is value-independent.
+t, alive, how = session(["put h 0x%x, 0x0003" % (PCC2 + o) for _, o in BLOCKS12])
+row("latch all", t.count(DECL), 12,
+    "one wide write per BLOCK -> TWELVE announces: bit CARDINALITY, not one pair")
+row("latch all lv", how, "exit0", "...and the host survived all twelve blocks")
+

+#  The block above proves each odd arm ANNOUNCES; it cannot see a guard that announces AND
+#  then lets the halfword land (the M_FALL shape -- decline nulled, guard kept: clause (d)).
+#  PRETABLE Section 2 had disclosed that as "a sentence owed".  The measuring seat asked
+#  whether a disclosure can discharge a floor clause; it cannot, or every breach becomes a
+#  sentence.  So each of the four arms gets the channel its body offers:
+#    SCCICR/SCCRX/SCSIICR store `idata & mask' then reassert_interrupts(): set 0x1f by

#!/bin/bash
#  pcc2widthkills pass-2 repair table -- the IPL/MASK rows.  Runs after the panels clear.
#    BASE          78/78 owed
#    M_DELETE_MASK MASK guard removed        -> rb mask reads 0xbb (the twin of M_DELETE)   was a 60/60 sweep
#    M_KILL_IPL    IPL width kill reinstated -> hrd ipl exit1 (GUEST-reachable, even)       was a 60/60 sweep
#    M_KILL_MASK   MASK width kill reinstated-> op mask exit1 (operator)                   was a 60/60 sweep
#    M_DELETE      (control) still reds rb genctl -- the repair perturbed nothing
#  PER-ARM (pass 2, grok): only GENCTL had a readback, so a guard edited at ONE arm escaped.
#    M_DELETE_TXICR  TXICR guard removed      -> rb t2icr 0x10 (halfword lands: 0xaab8 & 0x17)
#    M_LENGT2_TXICR  `len != 1' -> `len > 2'  -> rb t2icr 0x10, rb t2icr dcl 0
#    M_LENGT2_TXCTL  same at TXCTL            -> rb t2ctl 0x0  (0xaab8 & 3)
#    M_LENGT2_SCCTX  same at SCCTX            -> rb scctx 0x18 (0xaab8 & 0x1f)
#    M_LENGT2_SCCERR same at SCCERR           -> hwr sccerr dcl 0 (value-identical arm: message only)
#  THE BUILDER'S SWEEPS not already above (Opus, pass 2, table of 11):
#    M_ALIAS_SCCTX   SCCTX declines on SCCRX's bit (ONE char) -> latch all 11 (a collision drops the count)
#    M_LENGT2_MASK   MASK `len != 1' -> `len > 2'        -> rb mask 0xbb (halfword lands)
#    M_FALL_MASK     MASK announces then falls through   -> rb mask 0xbb
#    M_IDATA_GENCTL guard operand len -> idata > 0xff (PRE-TABLE yield) -> rb genctl lo 0x22, latch all 11
set -u
G=/mnt/c/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL; SEC=$G/GXEMUL-SEC; T=/tmp/gxpcc2b; F=src/devices/dev_pcc2.c
OUT=/tmp/killpcc2b; PROBE=pcc2_width_probe.py
say() { echo "[$(date +%H:%M:%S)] $*"; }
die() { echo "*** ABORT: $*"; exit 9; }
rm -rf "$T" "$OUT"; mkdir -p "$OUT"
cp -a "$G/build" "$T" || die "clone failed"
cmp -s "$T/$F" "$SEC/$F" || { cp "$SEC/$F" "$T/$F"; say "  clone's $F refreshed from SEC (comment-only change since build/ was built)"; }
cp "$SEC/regress/$PROBE" "$OUT/frozen_probe.py" || die "cannot freeze probe"
say "  instrument frozen: $(wc -c < "$OUT/frozen_probe.py") bytes"
build() { cd "$T" || die cd; rm -f src/devices/dev_pcc2.o; make -j4 > "$OUT/build_$1.log" 2>&1; [ "$T/gxemul" -nt "$T/$F" ] || die "$1: binary not newer than source"; }
score() {
    cd "$SEC/regress" || die cd
    python3 -u "$OUT/frozen_probe.py" "$T/gxemul" "$1" > "$OUT/$1.out" 2>&1
    grep -q PCC2W_PROBE_DONE "$OUT/$1.out" || die "$1: NO COMPLETION SENTINEL"
    say "  $1 [$2]: $(grep -m1 PCC2W_RESULT "$OUT/$1.out")"
    grep -m1 PCC2W_RED "$OUT/$1.out" | sed 's/PCC2W_RED=/      RED: /' || true
}
mut() {
    echo "$F  mutant $1" > "$T/.MUTANT"; cp "$SEC/$F" "$T/$F"
    python3 - "$T/$F" "$3" <<'PYEOF'
import io, sys
p, body = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]; s = io.open(p, encoding='utf-8').read()
ns = {'s': s}; exec(body, ns); io.open(p, 'w', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n').write(ns['s'])
PYEOF
    [ $? -eq 0 ] || die "$1: mutator failed"
    cmp -s "$T/$F" "$SEC/$F" && die "$1: file UNCHANGED after mutation"
    build "$1"; score "$1" "$2"
}

NO-GO

The detector has a one-character escape in the latch logic. In [dev_pcc2.c](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/devices/dev_pcc2.c:399), delete `|`:

```c
d->warned = (1u << bit);
```

This compiles but forgets previously announced blocks. An `A, B, A` sequence announces A twice, violating “once per block per instance.”

All 96 rows remain green:

- `latch same` uses `A, A`; the second A is still suppressed.
- `latch two` uses `A, B`; both still announce.
- `latch all` and `latch all w` visit each bit once, so both still count 12.
- Every other session is fresh and has at most one decline per block.

Thus the specifically requested row expected to stay green is `latch same`, and the expected overall result is 96/96. Add an `A, B, A` row expecting one A announcement, enumerate an OVERWRITE shape, and measure this mutant before proceeding.

I swept 108 block/shape cells—12 blocks across DEL, LEN, LEN2, KILL, ALIAS, FALL, SWAP, GROW and RDDECL—plus the T1CTL/T1ICR label paths.

- All 28 S cells check out from source: DEL’s eight, LEN’s seven, SCCERR/LEN2, KILL’s nine, and FALL at TxCTL, TxICR and SCCTX. The storage outcomes for `0xaab8` are VECBASE `0xb0`, TxCTL `0x00`, TxICR `0x10`, SCCICR/SCCTX `0x18`, and SCCRX/SCSIICR `0x98`. The print/message-only arms have the stated channels.
- SCCERR/FALL is genuinely equivalent in this model: initialization leaves SCCERR zero, its sole assignment writes zero, and no tick or interrupt path makes it nonzero.
- The six odd-only RDDECL cells are unreachable through the identified guest/debugger paths. The six even block cells are covered; the extra seventh row is SCCERR’s word read. T1CTL/T1ICR share guards with their even T2 labels.
- The exact ALIAS, SWAP, LEN2 and GROW C/W/A claims are sound. Their sessions contain no benign diagnostic matching `DECL`. But those cardinality rows do not establish latch-set persistence, as the overwrite escape demonstrates.

There is another domain omission if “decline wide writes” includes every handler input: no row uses `put d`, despite the contract explicitly saying it reaches with `len == 8`. A predicate declining only lengths 2 and 4 passes every current row while accepting an eight-byte write. Add a dword cardinality row or explicitly exclude length 8.

The accept-side record still overclaims. [The five individual accept rows](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/pcc2_width_probe.py:395) plus `acc all` prove GROW detection, no decline, and survival; `acc all` does not prove that every byte write reaches its arm body or is read back. In particular:

- PSCALEADJ and IPL are print-only, with no legal-byte row asserting their prints.
- SCCERR begins and remains zero, so `acc sccerr` cannot prove its clear assignment executed.

Accordingly, “byte writes … still land, read back through each arm’s own mask” is not carried as written. Narrow it to the measured GROW/no-decline property, add message rows for PSCALEADJ/IPL, and disclose SCCERR’s byte-side equivalence.

Record defects:

- “Product diff is comments only” is false: the diff adds a compile-time typedef, as visible in [dev_pcc2.c](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/devices/dev_pcc2.c:132). The same false wording appears at [CHANGELOG.md](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/CHANGELOG.md:3).
- The contract’s opening says twelve `fatal(); exit(1)` blocks ended the host. Two were warn-once latches and did not end it; the corrected product comment contradicts the contract.
- “Twelve blocks × six shapes” is stale: the displayed table has eight shapes, with RDDECL described separately as the ninth.
- PRETABLE Section 6’s `put b`/M_GROW prediction is explicitly refuted by its own measurements. Section 7 honestly records the escape and correction, so retaining the prediction as chronological history is acceptable.
- The PRETABLE split itself is honest: Section 0 admits that the original mtime proof was destroyed. Restoring the content does not restore that proof, and the record does not claim otherwise. The brief’s heading saying “Sections 5 and 6” is stale because the file also contains Section 7.

OPTION-SET COMPLETENESS remains N/A; no disposition choice is being made.
